Tattoo Studio Essentials Three essentials for a tattoo studio workflow
Personal booking forms, client history and tattoo projects — connected in one place.
Try for free
Personal booking forms Client base Tattoo projects
A client request should not stay lost in messages. InkLinka helps collect the idea, references, contact details, deposit, client history and project progress in one workflow.
Essential 01
Personal booking forms
Each artist in the studio can have a personal booking link or a booking form on the studio website.
Clients can choose available dates and time, describe the tattoo idea, attach references and pay a deposit before the studio reviews the request.
The design of each form and link can be adjusted individually, so the booking page can match the artist or studio style.
Personal artist link
Available dates and time
Custom form design
References
Deposit
Automatic client, project and session creation
The studio receives a ready request. InkLinka can convert it into a session, create the connected tattoo project and save the client in the database automatically.
Personal link to booked session
Artist form, deposit and automatic records

Essential 03
Tattoo projects
Each project should be connected to a client.
The project record shows the stage, references, notes, sessions and the work that still needs attention.
Project stage
Session history
References
Notes
Finished work
Next work
This helps the studio understand not only new requests, but also unfinished or repeat work with existing clients.

How it works
From request to project
The important details move through the same workflow, so the request does not become separate from the calendar, client or project.

Below you will find tattoo care recommendations — following these rules is important for proper healing and maintaining the quality of the work.\n\n1. FIRST HOURS\n\nIf the tattoo is covered with film (including second skin), do not remove it for the time recommended by the artist.\n\nIf the film is regular — remove it 2–4 hours after the session.\n\n2. HYGIENE\n\nAfter removing the film, gently wash the tattoo with warm water and mild antibacterial soap.\n\nPat dry with a disposable paper towel (do not rub).\n\nWash the tattoo 3–4 times a day for the first 5–7 days.\n\n3. MOISTURIZING\n\nApply a thin layer of recommended healing product 3–4 times a day.\n\nDo not use alcohol, hydrogen peroxide, petroleum jelly, or greasy creams.\n\n4. WATER\n\nShowers are allowed, but avoid prolonged steaming.\n\nBaths, pools, saunas, steam rooms, and open water are prohibited until complete healing (usually 3–4 weeks).\n\n5. CLOTHING\n\nWear clean, loose clothing made of natural fabrics.\n\nAvoid friction and pressure on the tattooed area.\n\n6. WHAT NOT TO DO\n\nDo not scratch or pick at scabs or peeling.\n\nDo not cover the tattoo with tight bandages unnecessarily.\n\nDo not apply makeup or sunscreen until fully healed.\n\n7. SUN\n\nAvoid direct sunlight until fully healed.\n\nAfter healing, use sunscreen SPF 50+ to preserve color.\n\n8. WHEN TO SEE A DOCTOR\n\nIf you notice increasing redness, severe swelling, pus discharge, sharp pain, or fever — consult a doctor.\n\nIf you have any questions about healing, please contact us — we are always ready to help.\n\nBest regards,\n{studio_name}\n{artist_name}74:T5e9,Hello, {client_name}!\n\nThank you for visiting our studio. Below are recommendations for piercing care — proper care reduces the risk of complications and speeds up healing.\n\n1. HYGIENE\n\nWash your hands thoroughly before any contact with the piercing.\n\nTreat the piercing 2 times a day with sterile saline solution or a product recommended by the artist.\n\nDo not use alcohol, hydrogen peroxide, iodine, or aggressive antiseptics.\n\n2. CARE\n\nGently clean the piercing area from discharge without rotating the jewelry.\n\nDo not remove or change the jewelry until complete primary healing.\n\nAvoid pressure, snags, and trauma to the piercing area.\n\n3. WATER\n\nShowers are allowed.\n\nAvoid pools, saunas, steam rooms, open water, and sea bathing for 3–4 weeks (or longer — depending on the type of piercing).\n\n4. CLOTHING AND EXTERNAL FACTORS\n\nAvoid tight clothing, friction, and pressure on the piercing area.\n\nTry not to touch the piercing unnecessarily.\n\nBe careful when sleeping, exercising, and using headphones/helmets (if applicable).\n\n5. HEALING\n\nMild swelling, redness, and clear discharge in the first days are normal reactions.\n\nComplete healing can take from several weeks to several months depending on the piercing area.\n\n6. WHEN TO SEE A DOCTOR\nIf you notice increasing pain, pronounced swelling, pus discharge, severe redness, or fever — consult a doctor.\n\nIf you have any questions about care or healing, please contact us — we are always ready to help.\n\nBest regards,\n{studio_name}\n{artist_name}75:Ta5f,I, the undersigned, voluntarily consent to the tattoo/piercing procedure and confirm the following:\n\n1.
